const list = document.getElementById("tickets");
new EventSource("/live").onmessage = (e) => {
  const li = document.createElement("li");
  li.className = "new";
  li.textContent = JSON.parse(e.data).title;
  list.prepend(li);
};

  e.preventDefault();

  e.target.reset();

};
